# Batmobile particle systems - the semantic pass (G1-6)

Generated by `Tools/scripts/grab_particles.py` from all **206** `ParticleSystem` exports in
the Batmobile packages. Every row is read from the cooked graph

`system -> emitter -> LOD level -> {required, spawn, modules[], type data}`,

so the *kind* of each effect (sprite, mesh, beam, trail, light, decal) is a fact of the
package, and the material / module inventory is what the game actually loads.

| fact | value |
|---|---|
| systems | **206** |
| emitters | **1433** (1096 with LOD levels, 337 stubs) |
| LOD levels | **1107** |
| distinct materials | **63** |
| emitters with serialised min/max vectors | **851** |

## Roles

| role | systems |
|---|---|
| light emitter | 98 |
| sprite particles | 29 |
| mesh particles | 28 |
| beam / tracer | 26 |
| burnout smoke | 17 |
| fear gas | 4 |
| decal | 3 |
| trail | 1 |

Roles are assigned from the graph first (type-data module, then module set), and only
fall back to the system name when the graph says only "sprite".

## Distribution values

`FRawDistributionFloat` is cooked to `Op` + `LookupTableNumElements` + a `LookupTable` array,
and the array carries the values - `Op == 2` uniform as `[min, max]` per chunk, a repeated
**1079 of 1107** LOD levels resolve a lifetime and **1017** resolve a spawn rate or rate; the remaining modules carry no table at all (the emitter inherits the required module's default). Values below are the cooked numbers, unscaled.
